## fade_sure
Fade the sure thing: in weather markets that end within a day, buys the CHEAP side (2-10c) when the other side looks 'sure' at 90-98c. Most bets lose; the few that win pay 10-50x. Real order-book fills. $25 paper

## ladder
Ladder pairs inside Polymarket: two markets of one event where the stricter one can only say YES if the looser one does (higher strike, earlier deadline). Buys NO on the stricter + YES on the looser when both cost under $1 after fees: pays at least $1, or $2 if only the looser one comes true. Same site and rules, so no cross-site mismatch. $100 paper

**Proof status:** Not proven yet: no finished sets. At ~2.0c edge we need about 114 finished sets with 0 short payouts to be 90% sure.

No open pairs.

## maker
Maker (added 2026-11-08): gets paid Polymarket's liquidity rewards for leaving buy and sell orders near the middle price, and pays for every time someone better informed picks those orders off. Fills from the real trade tape, back of the queue. $200 paper

Rewards earned **$34.87**, buying and selling (getting picked off, spreads, leftovers) **-20.50**. It only works if the first number stays bigger.

## sharp_pinnacle
Sharp bookie price: buys a Polymarket game outcome (soccer, NBA/NHL/NFL, baseball, esports, MMA) when it costs at least 3c less than Pinnacle's odds say it's worth, after removing Pinnacle's margin and paying the fee. Pre-game only, holds to the end. Real order-book fills. $100 paper

No open positions.

## Real money check

Every paper trade (5 shares) is also priced at bigger stakes on the same live order book: a limit order that never pays more than the best price + 2c, the bigger fee, and only what fits. Selling early walks the real bids. This is the number that matters for the $300-500 in January. Profit per $100 on finished trades, paper (5 shares) vs each stake:

## Data check

Sanity checks on the bot's own numbers after every run: money adds up, every fill was a real order-book fill, no track holds both sides of a market, nothing stuck unpaid, and no suspiciously perfect records. Last check 2026-11-09T14:52:03Z.

- **consensus_3** (both_sides): holds both sides of 1 market(s), e.g. us-announces-end-of-iranian-blockade-by-november-1
- **maker** (maker_rewards_high): modelled rewards ~$20/day per $100: unverified, likely optimistic
